| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 identifies the Eligibility Date and makes it available for transaction effective date population | Verify an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment file containing the Eligibility Date in Loop 2000 DTP*356 and Loop 2000 DTP*348 is processed end to end, the first Eligibility Date occurrence is retained when multiple Eligibility Date occurrences are present, and the identified value is available for transaction effective date population in Facets. | AR PASSE test data is prepared for one member in the CRT environment with a valid NM1 IL member SSN, unique ISA13 GS06 GE02 IEA02 control numbers for this execution, and an inbound daily AR PASSE enrollment file named TP028433_E2026031_123456789.834.
